<div class="td-module-meta-info">
<h3 class="entry-title td-module-title"><a href="https://www.actionforex.com/articles/forex-trading/26475-inside-the-forex-market-searching-for-black-swans/" rel="bookmark" title="Inside the Forex Market: Searching for Black Swans?">Inside the Forex Market: Searching for Black Swans?</a></h3>
<div class="td-editor-date">
<span class="td-author-date">
<a class="td-author-photo" href="https://www.actionforex.com/author/orbex/"><img alt="" class="avatar avatar-96 photo td-animation-stack-type0-1" height="96" src="https://secure.gravatar.com/avatar/743db7845d908f2a8077473c3816a36a?s=96&amp;d=mm&amp;r=g" srcset="https://secure.gravatar.com/avatar/743db7845d908f2a8077473c3816a36a?s=192&amp;d=mm&amp;r=g 2x" width="96"/></a> <span class="td-post-author-name"><a href="https://www.actionforex.com/author/orbex/">Orbex</a> <span>-</span> </span> <span class="td-post-date"><time class="entry-date updated td-module-date" datetime="2017-07-11T03:02:18+00:00">Jul 11, 03:02 GMT</time></span> </span>
</div>
<div class="td-excerpt">The term itself refers to an event which occurs well beyond the boundaries of what is typically expected and, therefore, represents a catastrophic surprise. The unexpected and random nature of black swan events makes them extremely difficult to predict. Given the difficult nature of anticipating these events, Taleb suggests it is important for people to always be aware that such dramatic events can happen and to focus on their risk management, protecting themselves. </div>

<div class="td-module-meta-info">
<h3 class="entry-title td-module-title"><a href="https://www.actionforex.com/articles/trading-psychology/26658-five-simple-trading-lessons-from-hell-s-kitchen/" rel="bookmark" title="Five Simple Trading Lessons from Hell’s Kitchen">Five Simple Trading Lessons from Hell’s Kitchen</a></h3>
<div class="td-editor-date">
<span class="td-author-date">
<a class="td-author-photo" href="https://www.actionforex.com/author/orbex/"><img alt="" class="avatar avatar-96 photo td-animation-stack-type0-1" height="96" src="https://secure.gravatar.com/avatar/743db7845d908f2a8077473c3816a36a?s=96&amp;d=mm&amp;r=g" srcset="https://secure.gravatar.com/avatar/743db7845d908f2a8077473c3816a36a?s=192&amp;d=mm&amp;r=g 2x" width="96"/></a> <span class="td-post-author-name"><a href="https://www.actionforex.com/author/orbex/">Orbex</a> <span>-</span> </span> <span class="td-post-date"><time class="entry-date updated td-module-date" datetime="2017-07-11T16:21:14+00:00">Jul 11, 16:21 GMT</time></span> </span>
</div>
<div class="td-excerpt">Hell's Kitchen, one of Chef Gordon Ramsay's many reality TV shows is quite an entertaining show to watch. Chefs face off to win a grand prize of running their own restaurants at the end. However, the path to success isn't easy as the contestants are truly put to hell. From having to deal with their peers to putting their differences aside and working as a team, Hell's Kitchen simply draws the viewer into it. </div>
<div class="td-read-more">
<a href="https://www.actionforex.com/articles/trading-psychology/26658-five-simple-trading-lessons-from-hell-s-kitchen/">Read more</a>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class="td-module-container td-category-pos-">
<div class="td-module-meta-info">
<h3 class="entry-title td-module-title"><a href="https://www.actionforex.com/articles/trading-psychology/32227-5-common-trading-mistakes-and-how-to-avoid-them/" rel="bookmark" title="5 Common Trading Mistakes and How to Avoid Them">5 Common Trading Mistakes and How to Avoid Them</a></h3>
<div class="td-editor-date">
<a class="td-post-category" href="https://www.actionforex.com/category/articles/trading-psychology/">Trading Psychology</a>
<span class="td-author-date">
<a class="td-author-photo" href="https://www.actionforex.com/author/pepperstone/"><img alt="" class="avatar avatar-96 photo td-animation-stack-type0-1" height="96" src="https://secure.gravatar.com/avatar/d4557daefabe4dc5bca5934684623ca9?s=96&amp;d=mm&amp;r=g" srcset="https://secure.gravatar.com/avatar/d4557daefabe4dc5bca5934684623ca9?s=192&amp;d=mm&amp;r=g 2x" width="96"/></a> <span class="td-post-author-name"><a href="https://www.actionforex.com/author/pepperstone/">Pepperstone</a> <span>-</span> </span> <span class="td-post-date"><time class="entry-date updated td-module-date" datetime="2017-08-06T18:09:40+00:00">Aug 06, 18:09 GMT</time></span> </span>
</div>
<div class="td-excerpt">Forex Trading is definitely not rocket science. Simple and subtle guidelines can allow aspiring traders to progress up the learning curve and become consistent in a relatively short period of time. These very guidelines have worked for centuries. Back in the early 1900s, Jesse Livermore used the exact same guidelines to make his fortune. </div> </div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="td_module_flex td_module_flex_4 td_module_wrap td-animation-stack">
<div class="td-module-container td-category-pos-">
<div class="td-module-meta-info">
<h3 class="entry-title td-module-title"><a href="https://www.actionforex.com/articles/trading-psychology/119010-how-to-simplify-your-forex-trading/" rel="bookmark" title="How to Simplify your Forex Trading">How to Simplify your Forex Trading</a></h3>
<div class="td-editor-date">
<a class="td-post-category" href="https://www.actionforex.com/category/articles/trading-psychology/">Trading Psychology</a>
<span class="td-author-date">
<a class="td-author-photo" href="https://www.actionforex.com/author/pepperstone/"><img alt="" class="avatar avatar-96 photo td-animation-stack-type0-1" height="96" src="https://secure.gravatar.com/avatar/d4557daefabe4dc5bca5934684623ca9?s=96&amp;d=mm&amp;r=g" srcset="https://secure.gravatar.com/avatar/d4557daefabe4dc5bca5934684623ca9?s=192&amp;d=mm&amp;r=g 2x" width="96"/></a> <span class="td-post-author-name"><a href="https://www.actionforex.com/author/pepperstone/">Pepperstone</a> <span>-</span> </span> <span class="td-post-date"><time class="entry-date updated td-module-date" datetime="2018-08-05T15:06:11+00:00">Aug 05, 15:06 GMT</time></span> </span>
</div>
<div class="td-excerpt">It’s entirely possible to trade profitably using just our eyes, charts, and grey matter as “hunting” for setups can cause more pain than gain....</div> </div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="td_module_flex td_module_flex_4 td_module_wrap td-animation-stack">
<div class="td-module-container td-category-pos-">
<div class="td-module-meta-info">
<h3 class="entry-title td-module-title"><a href="https://www.actionforex.com/articles/trading-psychology/111087-what-can-we-learn-from-successful-traders/" rel="bookmark" title="What Can We Learn from Successful Traders?">What Can We Learn from Successful Traders?</a></h3>
<div class="td-editor-date">
<a class="td-post-category" href="https://www.actionforex.com/category/articles/trading-psychology/">Trading Psychology</a>
<span class="td-author-date">
<a class="td-author-photo" href="https://www.actionforex.com/author/icmarkets/"><img alt="" class="avatar avatar-96 photo td-animation-stack-type0-1" height="96" src="https://secure.gravatar.com/avatar/e7292a219ef421b2ec6ab1d878993406?s=96&amp;d=mm&amp;r=g" srcset="https://secure.gravatar.com/avatar/e7292a219ef421b2ec6ab1d878993406?s=192&amp;d=mm&amp;r=g 2x" width="96"/></a> <span class="td-post-author-name"><a href="https://www.actionforex.com/author/icmarkets/">IC Markets</a> <span>-</span> </span> <span class="td-post-date"><time class="entry-date updated td-module-date" datetime="2018-07-09T15:39:22+00:00">Jul 09, 15:39 GMT</time></span> </span>
</div>
<div class="td-excerpt">Although at times trading can seem like banging one’s head against a wall, there is light at the end of the tunnel.

There are traders...</div> </div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="td_module_flex td_module_flex_4 td_module_wrap td-animation-stack">
<div class="td-module-container td-category-pos-">
<div class="td-module-meta-info">
<h3 class="entry-title td-module-title"><a href="https://www.actionforex.com/articles/trading-psychology/26921-managing-greed-in-forex-trading/" rel="bookmark" title="Managing Greed in Forex Trading">Managing Greed in Forex Trading</a></h3>
<div class="td-editor-date">
<a class="td-post-category" href="https://www.actionforex.com/category/articles/trading-psychology/">Trading Psychology</a>
<span class="td-author-date">
<a class="td-author-photo" href="https://www.actionforex.com/author/vantagefx/"><img alt="" class="avatar avatar-96 photo td-animation-stack-type0-1" height="96" src="https://secure.gravatar.com/avatar/1891b32560f603a330b2cc9bed93757e?s=96&amp;d=mm&amp;r=g" srcset="https://secure.gravatar.com/avatar/1891b32560f603a330b2cc9bed93757e?s=192&amp;d=mm&amp;r=g 2x" width="96"/></a> <span class="td-post-author-name"><a href="https://www.actionforex.com/author/vantagefx/">Vantage FX</a> <span>-</span> </span> <span class="td-post-date"><time class="entry-date updated td-module-date" datetime="2017-07-12T17:54:38+00:00">Jul 12, 17:54 GMT</time></span> </span>
</div>
<div class="td-excerpt">If you want to be a successful forex trader, greed is probably the biggest obstacle you'll have to overcome. If you try to get rich on every trade, you'll more than likely end up blowing your account - slow and steady wins the race. It's the great paradox that all traders face - if you want to get rich quickly, you have to do it slowly. </div> </div>
</div>
</div>

<h3 class="entry-title td-module-title"><a href="https://www.actionforex.com/articles/trading-strategies/41454-scalping-how-to-opportunistically-trade-the-fx-market/" rel="bookmark" title="Scalping: How to Opportunistically Trade the FX Market">Scalping: How to Opportunistically Trade the FX Market</a></h3>
<div class="td-editor-date">

<span class="td-author-date">
<a class="td-author-photo" href="https://www.actionforex.com/author/orbex/"><img alt="" class="avatar avatar-96 photo td-animation-stack-type0-1" height="96" src="https://secure.gravatar.com/avatar/743db7845d908f2a8077473c3816a36a?s=96&amp;d=mm&amp;r=g" srcset="https://secure.gravatar.com/avatar/743db7845d908f2a8077473c3816a36a?s=192&amp;d=mm&amp;r=g 2x" width="96"/></a> <span class="td-post-author-name"><a href="https://www.actionforex.com/author/orbex/">Orbex</a> <span>-</span> </span> <span class="td-post-date"><time class="entry-date updated td-module-date" datetime="2017-09-08T18:42:47+00:00">Sep 08, 18:42 GMT</time></span> </span>

<div class="td-excerpt">Scalping is an area of Forex trading that always appeals to new traders because of the potential to make big profits, quickly. However, with the high level of reward also comes a high level of risk, so it is super important that you take the time to properly understand scalping before applying it to live markets. As ever we are going to take things slow and steady and make sure that we cover everything so that you are really comfortable. </div> </div>

<h3 class="entry-title td-module-title"><a href="https://www.actionforex.com/articles/trading-strategies/43280-how-to-trade-forex-with-swing-trading-strategies/" rel="bookmark" title="How to Trade Forex with Swing Trading Strategies">How to Trade Forex with Swing Trading Strategies</a></h3>
<div class="td-editor-date">
<a class="td-post-category" href="https://www.actionforex.com/category/articles/trading-strategies/">Trading Strategies</a>
<span class="td-author-date">
<a class="td-author-photo" href="https://www.actionforex.com/author/pepperstone/"><img alt="" class="avatar avatar-96 photo td-animation-stack-type0-1" height="96" src="https://secure.gravatar.com/avatar/d4557daefabe4dc5bca5934684623ca9?s=96&amp;d=mm&amp;r=g" srcset="https://secure.gravatar.com/avatar/d4557daefabe4dc5bca5934684623ca9?s=192&amp;d=mm&amp;r=g 2x" width="96"/></a> <span class="td-post-author-name"><a href="https://www.actionforex.com/author/pepperstone/">Pepperstone</a> <span>-</span> </span> <span class="td-post-date"><time class="entry-date updated td-module-date" datetime="2017-09-17T15:16:00+00:00">Sep 17, 15:16 GMT</time></span> </span>
</div>
<div class="td-excerpt">Swing Trading is a mid term trading strategy often utilised by FX and CFD traders. Swing Trading positions may be kept open for just a few hours, but Swing Traders can also choose to keep a trade open for much longer, under the right market conditions. </div> </div>
</div>

function pivots(form) {
high1 = form.high.value * 1;
low1 = form.low.value * 1;
close1 = form.close.value * 1;

pivot = Math.round(((high1 + low1 + close1) / 3) * Math.pow(10,4)) / 10000;
r1 = Math.round((2 * pivot - low1) * Math.pow(10,4)) / 10000;
s1 = Math.round((2 * pivot - high1) * Math.pow(10,4)) / 10000;
r2 = Math.round((pivot - s1 + r1) * Math.pow(10,4)) / 10000;
s2 = Math.round((pivot - (r1 - s1)) * Math.pow(10,4)) / 10000;
r3 = Math.round((2 * (pivot - low1) + high1) * Math.pow(10,4)) / 10000;
s3 = Math.round((low1 - (2 * (high1 - pivot))) * Math.pow(10,4)) / 10000;

form.result6.value = r3;
form.result.value = r2;

form.result2.value = r1;
form.result3.value = pivot;
form.result4.value = s1;
form.result5.value = s2;
form.result7.value = s3;
}

function retup(form) {
A = form.A.value * 1;
B = form.B.value * 1;

dist=Math.round((B-A)*Math.pow(10,4))/10000;
ret1=Math.round((B-0.236*dist)*Math.pow(10,4))/10000;
ret2=Math.round((B-0.382*dist)*Math.pow(10,4))/10000;
ret3=Math.round((B-0.5*dist)*Math.pow(10,4))/10000;
ret4=Math.round((B-0.618*dist)*Math.pow(10,4))/10000;
ret5=Math.round((B-0.786*dist)*Math.pow(10,4))/10000;
ret6=Math.round((B-1.382*dist)*Math.pow(10,4))/10000;
ret7=Math.round((B-1.618*dist)*Math.pow(10,4))/10000;


form.result1.value = ret1;
form.result2.value = ret2;
form.result3.value = ret3;
form.result4.value = ret4;
form.result5.value = ret5;
form.result6.value = ret6;
form.result7.value = ret7;
}

function projup(form) {
A = form.A.value * 1;
B = form.B.value * 1;
C = form.C.value * 1;

dist=Math.round((B-A)*Math.pow(10,4))/10000;
ret1=Math.round((C+0.618*dist)*Math.pow(10,4))/10000;
ret2=Math.round((C+1*dist)*Math.pow(10,4))/10000;
ret3=Math.round((C+1.382*dist)*Math.pow(10,4))/10000;
ret4=Math.round((C+1.618*dist)*Math.pow(10,4))/10000;
ret5=Math.round((C+2*dist)*Math.pow(10,4))/10000;
ret6=Math.round((C+2.618*dist)*Math.pow(10,4))/10000;


form.result1.value = ret1;
form.result2.value = ret2;
form.result3.value = ret3;
form.result4.value = ret4;
form.result5.value = ret5;
form.result6.value = ret6;
}

function retdown(form) {
A = form.A.value * 1;
B = form.B.value * 1;

dist=Math.round((A-B)*Math.pow(10,4))/10000;
ret1=Math.round((B+0.236*dist)*Math.pow(10,4))/10000;
ret2=Math.round((B+0.382*dist)*Math.pow(10,4))/10000;
ret3=Math.round((B+0.5*dist)*Math.pow(10,4))/10000;
ret4=Math.round((B+0.618*dist)*Math.pow(10,4))/10000;
ret5=Math.round((B+0.786*dist)*Math.pow(10,4))/10000;
ret6=Math.round((B+1.382*dist)*Math.pow(10,4))/10000;
ret7=Math.round((B+1.618*dist)*Math.pow(10,4))/10000;


form.result1.value = ret1;
form.result2.value = ret2;
form.result3.value = ret3;
form.result4.value = ret4;
form.result5.value = ret5;
form.result6.value = ret6;
form.result7.value = ret7;
}


function projdown(form) {
A = form.A.value * 1;
B = form.B.value * 1;
C = form.C.value * 1;

dist=Math.round((A-B)*Math.pow(10,4))/10000;
ret1=Math.round((C-0.618*dist)*Math.pow(10,4))/10000;
ret2=Math.round((C-1*dist)*Math.pow(10,4))/10000;
ret3=Math.round((C-1.382*dist)*Math.pow(10,4))/10000;
ret4=Math.round((C-1.618*dist)*Math.pow(10,4))/10000;
ret5=Math.round((C-2*dist)*Math.pow(10,4))/10000;
ret6=Math.round((C-2.618*dist)*Math.pow(10,4))/10000;


form.result1.value = ret1;
form.result2.value = ret2;
form.result3.value = ret3;
form.result4.value = ret4;
form.result5.value = ret5;
form.result6.value = ret6;
}
